We need speed. Direct G: For d positive: for i range, contribution p_d * (derivative). We can vectorize over d? For each d, update G[0:n-d] += p_d*(1 - h[d:n-d] - (h[:n-d-?]? let's derive):
For d>0:
Term A_d = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-d} h_i -∑h_i h_{i+d}. ∂G:
For i=0..n-1-d: +1 - h_{i+d} (as first). For i=d..n-1: -h_{i-d} (as second).
So for d>0:
G[0:n-d] += p*(1 - h[d:n]) (since h_{i+d} for i 0..n-1-d -> h[d:n])
G[d:n] += -p*h[0:n-d]
For d=0:
G += p*(1 - 2h)
For d<0, let s=-d>0. A_{-s}=∑_{i=s}^{n-1} h_i -∑h_i h_{i-s}. Derivative:
As first h_i for i=s..n-1: +1 - h_{i-s}; as second for i=0..n-1-s: -h_{i+s}.
So:
G[s:n] += p*(1 - h[0:n-s])
G[0:n-s] += -p*h[s:n]
This is O(n^2) in Python loops over d; if n=900 and 1000 iterations, 800M maybe too slow. But we can vectorize using cumulative? Actually total operations ~n^2. Direct G can be computed with FFT O(n log n), better.

Projected gradient algorithm:
```
def projected_gradient(h, T_schedule, iterations, rng, objective_fn?):
  n=len(h); target=n/2
  best_h=h.copy(); best_M=maxA(h)
  for it in range(iters):
    A = correlate_full(h)  # d order
    p = softmax(A,T)
    Graw = weighted_gradient(p,h)
    # line search on smooth/raw
    step = step0
    y = project(h - step*Graw, target)
    Mnew = maxA(y)
    while Mnew > M - tol and step > minstep:
       step *=0.5
       y = project(h - step*Graw, target)
       Mnew=maxA(y)
    if Mnew < M:
       h=y; M=Mnew
    else:
       # maybe try small random perturb + restart
       step=...
```

Direct gradient:
```
def grad_direct(p,h):
  n=len(h); G=np.zeros(n)
  G += p[0]*(1-2*h)? p index d=-N... d=0 at idx N.
  # d=0 idx N
  G += p[N]*(1.0 - 2.0*h)
  # d>0: s=1..N, idx=N+s
  for s in range(1,n):
      ps=p[N+s]
      if ps==0: continue
      m=n-s
      G[:m] += ps*(1.0 - h[s:])
      G[s:] += -ps*h[:m]
  # d<0: s=1..N, idx=N-s
  for s in range(1,n):
      ps=p[N-s]
      if ps==0: continue
      m=n-s
      G[s:] += ps*(1.0 - h[:m])
      G[:m] += -ps*h[s:]
  return G
```
Check for d<0: s=-d, A_{-s}: G[s:n] += p*(1 - h[0:n-s]); G[0:n-s] += -p*h[s:n]. Yes.

This direct gradient uses p raw weights sum1. Good.

FFT gradient for n>400:
```
def grad_fft(p,h):
 n=len(h); N=n-1; M=2*n-1
 # P length M index u=N+d
 P=np.zeros(M); P[n-1-n? wait d=-N..N -> u=0..M-1 map d=u-N. Since p index same 0..M-1. So P = p already? p order d=-N..N, index u=d+N. center at n-1. So P=p.
 P_rev=p[::-1]
 L=1; while L < M+n-1: L*=2
 f1=np.zeros(L); f1[:M]=P
 f2=np.zeros(L); f2[:n]=h
 conv=ifft(fft(f1)*fft(f2)).real
 Hminus=conv[N:N+n]  # N=n-1
 f1[:M]=P_rev; f1[M:]=0? We can create new.
 conv2=...
 Hplus=conv2[N:N+n]
 # L_j sums p over d=-j..N-j.
 prefix=np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(P)))
 starts=N - np.arange(n)  # 0? for j=0 start=N; j=N start=0
 ends=2*N - np.arange(n)  # j=0 end=2N=M-1; j=N end=N
 Lval = prefix[ends+1] - prefix[starts]
 return Lval - Hplus - Hminus
```
Let's test indices N=n-1. M=2N+1. starts: j=0 N, j=N 0. ends: j=0 2N=M-1, j=N N. prefix length M+1. Good. Hminus=conv1[N:N+n] from N to 2N? length n. In test n=2 N=1 conv length M+n-1=5? indices 0..4, conv[1:3] length2 -> Hminus for j=0,1. Good. Hplus same.
Need ensure P center at length? The convolution formula used P_center length M where index u=N+d. p order already u=0..M-1 (d=u-N). Good.

Projection:
```
def project_box_sum(v, target):
    # v in [maybe]; find mu
    lo = float(v.min()) - 1.0 - 1e-12
    hi = float(v.max()) + 1.0 + 1e-12
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(v-mid,0,1).sum()
       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    x=np.clip(v-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # if sum off, adjust robustly
    diff=target-x.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
       # distribute to non-boundary; binary fallback
       x=fix_sum(x,target)
    return x
```
Binary search because f(mid) decreasing in mid? If mid increases, clip decreases, sum decreases. If sum>target, need increase mid (lo=mid) to reduce. If sum<target, decrease mid (hi=mid). Good.
`fix_sum(h,target)` as previous iterative; but if target exactly integer and sum off by tiny, can do:

```
def obj_grad_penalty(a, n, target, lam, T, full=True):
   h = a if full else [a,a]
   value, G = smooth raw for h
   s = h.sum() (or a.sum()+a.sum())
```

We can define `L_BFGS_B runner`:
```
def run_lbgfs(x0, mode, T_list, time_limit, maxiter=2000, lam=1e3):
  target = len(x0)/2
  bounds=[(0,1)]*len
  init_step maybe
  current=x0; currentC=compute_c5_or_raw
  for T in T_list:
     t_start=time
     try:
       if mode=='full':
          fun = partial(penalty_obj_full, T=T, lam=lam, n=len(x0))
       else: ...
       res=minimize(fun, current, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxfun': None})
       new=fix_sum(clip(res.x), target)
       c=compute_c5_full(new, mode)
       if c<best: current=new
     except: pass
     # time if remaining small
```

### Function: compute_c5_from_h_full(h)
```
def compute_c5_full(h):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    dx=2.0/len(h)
    corr=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    return float(np.max(corr)*dx)
```

```
full=np.empty(2*len(a)); full[:m]=a; full[m:]=a; return compute_c5_full(full)
```

```
def penalty_obj_full(x, T, lam):
   target=x.size/2.0
   V,G=smooth_value_grad_full(x,T)
   delta=x.sum()-target
   val=V + lam*delta*delta
   grad=G + 2.0*lam*delta*np.ones(x.size)
   return val, grad
```

```
def penalty_obj_period(a, T, lam):

   delta=a.sum()-m/2.0
   val=V+lam*delta*delta
```

### Linear polish implementation
Top-level:
```
def grad_lag_full(d, h): # d order index 0..M-1, d_val = idx - (n-1)
   n=len(h); g=np.zeros(n); s = idx - (n-1)
   if s==0: g[:] = 1 - 2h
   elif s>0:
      m=n-s
      g[:m] = 1 - h[s:]
      g[s:] = -h[:m]
      # Need add not assign? For one lag if both conditions disjoint except? For s>0, first i=0..n-s-1, second i=s..n-1; disjoint? Yes first length n-s, second length n-s; indices overlap? first indices 0..n-s-1, second s..n-1. They are disjoint if s < n-s? They may overlap when s < n/2, first {0,1} second {1,2} overlap at index s? For s>0, first includes i=s if s≤n-s-1; second includes i=s. At i=s, h appears as first (partner s+s) and second (partner 0). Need add both! My direct gradient for d>0 adds to G[:n-s] and G[s:] (add to same entries for overlap). For single grad, must add both contributions:
      g[:m] += 1 - h[s:]
      g[s:] += -h[:m]
```

For s<0: sv=-s:
   g[sv:] += 1 - h[:n-sv]
   g[:n-sv] += -h[sv:]
This matches.

LP:
```
def linear_polish(h0, mode, K=40, rounds=20, time_limit):
   nvar=len(h0)
   target=nvar/2
   current=h0.copy(); bestC=eval_mode(current)
   A0 = corr_A_eval_mode(current) # in d order length Mfull; use selected indices, but if period full length=2m, constraints correspond full lags; variable a. We can select top by full A.
   # For each round:
   for round in range(rounds):
      A = corr_A_eval_mode(current); M=A.max()
      idx = np.argsort(A)[::-1][:K]
      # Build matrix rows and b
      A_ub=[]; b_ub=[]
      for d_idx in idx:
          d_val = d_idx - (full_n-1)
          if mode=='full': row=grad_lag_full(d_val, full_h)
          else: row=grad_lag_period(a, d_val)
          A_ub.append(np.concatenate([row, [-1.0]]))
          b_ub.append(-A[d_idx])  # row z - t <= -A0? Actually we want A0 + row·z <= t -> row z - t <= -A0. b=-A0.
      # But z bounds ensure feasibility. t variable unbounded.
      c = np.zeros(nvar+1); c[-1]=1
      A_eq=np.zeros((1,nvar+1)); A_eq[0,:nvar]=1
      b_eq=np.array([0.0])
      bounds=[(-current[i],1-current[i]) for i in range(nvar)] + [(None,None)]
      try linprog(c,A_ub=np.array(A_ub),b_ub=np.array(b_ub),A_eq,A_eq? bounds, method='highs')
```
Need if res success and t < M? The LP minimizes t; new h = current+z. But due linearization, exact might increase. Evaluate. If improves, current=new, bestC; else maybe no.
We should set trust radius by clipping z bounds? The LP will likely move to reduce active lags; z can be large. If exact worsens, we can try scaled z: for scale in [1,0.5,0.25], evaluate; if improve accept. Good.
```
      z=res.x[:-1]
      for scale in [1,0.5,0.25,0.1]:
          cand=fix_sum(clip(current+scale*z), target)
          C=eval_mode(cand)
          if C<bestC-1e-12: current=cand; bestC=C; break
```
If not improve, maybe break or reduce K? There is risk linprog constructing matrix expensive for high nvar (>400). Limit K small 20 for nvar>200. Or skip LP for nvar>400 to save. We'll implement threshold: full nvar<=400 or period m<=200.

### Exact fix_sum and clip
```
def fix_sum_exact(h, target):
    h=np.clip(h,0,1).copy()
    diff=target-h.sum()
    # iterative distribute
    for _ in range(100):
      if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
      if diff>0: idx=np.where(h<1.0-1e-15)[0]
      else: idx=np.where(h>1e-15)[0]
      if len(idx)==0: break
      # distribute evenly maybe many
      per=diff/len(idx)
      for i in idx: h[i]=np.clip(h[i]+per,0,1)
      diff=target-h.sum()
    # final one-index exact
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
      if diff>0: idx=np.where(h<1.0)[0] else idx=np.where(h>0)[0]
      if len(idx)>0:
          i=idx[0]
          h[i]=np.clip(h[i]+diff,0,1)
          diff=target-h.sum()
          # if still, distribute
    return h
```
If all at bounds and sum wrong (due floating), may not exact. But target integer, starting from fixed should maintain within 1e-12.

`fix_sum` previous uses `np.clip` vector per iteration; okay.

### Upsampling
```
def upsample_to(h_old,n_new):
   n_old=len(h_old)
   if n_old==n_new: return h_old.copy()
   # For even n and period, if n_new/n_old integer, we can do piecewise constant maybe better? Linear may sharpen.
   x_old=np.linspace(0,2,n_old,endpoint=False); x_new=np.linspace(0,2,n_new,endpoint=False)
   new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_old)
   return fix_sum_exact(new, n_new/2)
```

### Potential performance/numerical pitfalls
- `np.correlate` input dtype float64. A values raw positive? h(1-h) nonnegative, sums nonnegative. With floating, maybe tiny negative? max okay.
- `soft_value_weights`: if T very small, `np.exp((A-Amax)/T)` can underflow to all 0 except max; if s=0 set one-hot. For T=0.05 raw and A differences maybe 0, could underflow. Use stable: `x = (A-Amax)/T; xmax=x.max(); e=np.exp(x-xmax); if e.sum()==0: p one-hot`. Actually after subtract Amax, max x=0; exp(0)=1, so sum at least 1. So s>0. Good. If nan? no.
- `T` raw. Choose not too small to avoid almost one-hot causing discontinuities in gradient. T=0.1 may be okay. T=0.05 maybe. Use schedule [10,5,2,1,0.5,0.2,0.1].
- SLSQP with T=0.1 may fail; we can catch.
- L-BFGS-B `ftol` in SciPy: stops when norm gradient small; with raw objective 30 maybe. Set `'maxls': 20, 'maxcor': 20`.

Need import `functools`? We'll use.

### Top-level structure
```
import numpy as np
import time
import functools
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

# helpers as above

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1300,**kwargs):
   t0=time.time()
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   parent = globals().get('initial_h_values', None)
   if parent is None or np.size(parent)==0:
       h = fallback_init(180, rng)
   else:
       h = np.asarray(parent, float).copy()
   h = fix_sum_exact(h, len(h)/2)
   best_h = h; best_c=compute_c5_full(h)

   # define internal time check in main via if time...

   # Stage 0: quick L-BFGS/SLSQP on original n
   n0=len(h)
   if n0 and remaining>300:
       best_h,best_c = polish_full(n0, best_h, best_c, ... time_limit=min(remaining*0.25, 200))
       # period polish on parent periodized if n0 even
       if n0%2==0:
           a0 = 0.5*(h[:n0//2]+h[n0//2:])
           a0=fix_sum_exact(a0,n0//4)
           a,bestA=polish_period(n0//2,... time_limit=...)
           if bestA<best_c: best_h=np.concatenate([a,a]); ...
   # Stages for upsampling full
   current=best_h
   n=current size
   for factor in [2,4,8]:
       nnew=min(n*factor, 1080 maybe)
       if time remaining < allowance break
       # upsample current best (which may be previous n)
       x=upsample_to(current,nnew)
       # run L-BFGS/SLSQP full
       current2,c2=polish_full(x,nnew, time_limit)
       if c2<best_c: best=c2; current=current2
       # period polish from best full (periodized if length even)
       if nnew%2==0:
          a0 = periodize(current2, nnew)
          a,bestA=polish_period(nnew//2,...)
          if bestA<best_c: best_h=np.concatenate([a,a]); best_c=bestA; current=best_h
       # even n? If period solution length 2m gives full C, update current for next upsample? Use best_h.
   # final linear polish on best_h (full and periodized)
   if len(best_h) <= 500:
      c=linear_polish_full(best_h, time_limit=...)
      if c<best_c: update
      if even:
        a=periodize(best_h)
        cA=linear_polish_period(a,...)

   # Also final small perturbations SLSQP on best n? maybe included in polish functions.

```
Need implement `polish_full` and `polish_period` top-level to avoid closures. They should update best.

But the task says "Make all helper functions top level, no closures or lambdas". We can define many top-level functions.

Let's design `polish_full(h0, time_limit, seed, current_best_c)`. It returns (h_best, c_best). It will use L-BFGS-B and SLSQP, maybe a few perturbations. To respect time, check inside. It needs `T_list` and iterations based on n.

```
def polish_full(h0, time_limit, current_best_c, rng):
    n=len(h0); current=h0.copy(); bestC=current_best_c
    bounds=[(0.,1.)]*n
    target=n/2.
    t0=time.time()
    # L-BFGS-B stages
    maxiter = 2000 if n<=200 else (1000 if n<=500 else 400)
    T_list = [10,5,2,1,0.5,0.2,0.1]
    # maybe if time_limit small, fewer.
    for T in T_list:
       if elapsed>time_limit*0.7: break
       # run once with lam=1e3
       try:
         fun=functools.partial(penalty_obj_full, T=T, lam=1e3)
         res=minimize(fun, current, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8})
         cand=fix_sum_exact(np.clip(res.x,0,1), target)
         C=compute_c5_full(cand)
         if C<bestC:
            bestC=C; current=cand.copy()
       except: pass
       if elapsed>time_limit: break
    # SLSQP stages
    for T in [2,1,0.5,0.2]:
       if elapsed > time_limit*0.85: break
       maxiter_s = 800 if n<=200 else 300
       try:
         obj=functools.partial(slsqp_obj_full,T=T); jac=functools.partial(slsqp_jac_full,T=T)
         cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':functools.partial(eq_fun_n, n=n), 'jac':functools.partial(eq_jac_n, n=n)}]
         res=minimize(obj,current,args=(),jac=jac,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter_s,'ftol':1e-12})
         cand=fix_sum_exact(np.clip(res.x,0,1),target); C=...
         if C<bestC: update
       except: pass
    # Random perturbations quick L-BFGS-B T=0.5
    for r in range(3):
       if elapsed > time_limit: break
       x=current.copy(); x += rng.normal(0,0.03,n); x=fix_sum_exact(np.clip(x,0,1),target)
       # quick L-BFGS-B T=0.5 maxiter maxiter//2
    return current,bestC
```

`fix_sum_exact` target can be float. Implement robust:
```
def fix_sum_exact(h, target):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # first adjust by waterfill to get close
    v=h.copy()
    # We cannot use project because no v perturb; but can sort? Use iterative.
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0: mask=h<1.0
       else: mask=h>0.0
       if not mask.any(): break
       per=diff/mask.sum()
       h[mask]+=per
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # adjust one
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-15:
       if diff>0: mask=h<1.0
       else: mask=h>0.0
       if mask.any():
          idx=np.where(mask)[0]
          # distribute to exact if possible
          i=idx[0]
          amt = diff
          if diff>0:
              amt=min(amt,1-h[i])
          else:
              amt=max(amt,-h[i])
          h[i]+=amt
          diff=target-h.sum()
          for i in idx[1:]:
             if abs(diff)<=1e-15: break
             if diff>0: amt=min(diff,1-h[i]) else amt=max(diff,-h[i])
             h[i]+=amt; diff=target-h.sum()
    return h
```
If target n/2 and h clipped, iterative should converge. For all 0/1 and diff small impossible, but slight floating not.

`upsample_to`: If n_old==n_new copy and fix. If endpoint=False, linear interpolation. For values very near 0/1, maybe.

`resolve even`: In run, when choosing resolution, ensure even? For full general can be odd, but period not. We'll make all resolutions even: if nn%2==1: nn+=1. Starting parent if odd, leave? For full optimizations doesn't require even. For period, skip if odd. If best full odd and we want period, upsample to even first.

`make_cosine_period(m)`: if m<4, return full. Next power: `x=np.arange(m); a=np.clip(0.5+np.cos(2*np.pi*x/m),0,1); a=fix_sum_exact(a,m/2)`.
`make_random_binary`: if m even; if odd, target floor? For period we use even.

### `polish_full` details with time:
```
def polish_full(h0, time_limit, current_best_c, rng):
    n=len(h0); t0=time.time()
    if n<2 or time_limit<10: return h0.copy(), current_best_c
    current=h0.copy(); bestC=current_best_c
    target=n/2.0; bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    maxiter = 1200 if n<=200 else (700 if n<=450 else 350)
    T_list = []
    if time_limit > 200: T_list=[10,5,2,1,0.5,0.2,0.1]
    elif time_limit > 100: T_list=[5,2,1,0.5,0.2]
    else: T_list=[2,1,0.5]
    for T in T_list:
       if time.time()-t0 > time_limit*0.65: break
       try:
          fun = functools.partial(penalty_obj_full, T=T, lam=1e3)
          res = minimize(fun, current, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':10})
          cand = fix_sum_exact(np.clip(res.x,0,1), target)
          C = compute_c5_full(cand)
          if C < bestC - 1e-12:
              bestC = C; current = cand.copy()
       except Exception: pass
    # SLSQP
    if time.time()-t0 < time_limit*0.85:
       maxit_s = 600 if n<=200 else (350 if n<=450 else 200)
       for T in [2.0,1.0,0.5]:
          if time.time()-t0 > time_limit*0.9: break
          try:
             obj = functools.partial(slsqp_obj_full, T=T)
             jac = functools.partial(slsqp_jac_full, T=T)
             cons = [{'type':'eq','fun':functools.partial(eq_fun_full, n=n), 'jac':functools.partial(eq_jac_full, n=n)}]
             res = minimize(obj, current, jac=jac, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter': maxit_s, 'ftol':1e-12, 'disp':False})
             cand=fix_sum_exact(np.clip(res.x,0,1),target); C=...
             if C<bestC: update
          except Exception: pass
    # perturbations quick
    if time.time()-t0 < time_limit:
       for r in range(2):
          if time.time()-t0 > time_limit: break
          x=current + rng.normal(0,0.03,n)
          x=fix_sum_exact(np.clip(x,0,1),target)
          T=0.5
          maxit_p = max(50, maxiter//4)
          try:
             fun=functools.partial(penalty_obj_full,T=T,lam=1e3)
             res=minimize(fun,x,jac=True,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':maxit_p,'ftol':1e-11,'gtol':1e-7})
             cand=fix_sum...
             if improves update
          except: pass
    return current,bestC
```
`eq_fun_full(x,n)` and `eq_jac_full(x,n)` use no args extra. Partial `n=n` works.

```
def linear_polish_full(h0, time_limit, current_best_c):
   n=len(h0); if n>500 or time_limit<30: return h0, current_bestc
   t0=time.time(); current=h0.copy(); bestC=current_bestc; target=n/2
   K=40 if n<=250 else 20
   stalls=0
   for it in range(25):
      if time.time()-t0 > time_limit*0.9: break
      A=corr_A_full(current)
      idx=np.argsort(A)[::-1][:K]
      rows=[]; bubs=[]
      for d_idx in idx:
          row=grad_lag_int(d_idx,current)
          rows.append(row)
          bubs.append(-A[d_idx])
      A_ub=np.column_stack((np.vstack(rows), -np.ones(K)))
      b_ub=np.array(bubs)
      c=np.zeros(n+1); c[-1]=1.0
      A_eq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1.0
      b_eq=np.zeros(1)
      bounds=[(-float(current[i]), 1.0-float(current[i])) for i in range(n)]
      bounds.append((None,None))
      try:
         res=linprog(c,A_ub=A_ub,b_ub=b_ub,A_eq=A_eq,b_eq=b_eq,bounds=bounds,method='highs')
      except Exception:
         break
      if not res or not res.success:
         stalls +=1
      else:
         z=res.x[:-1]
         for scale in (1.0,0.5,0.2,0.1):
             cand=fix_sum_exact(np.clip(current + scale*z,0,1), target)
             C=compute_c5_full(cand)
             if C < bestC - 1e-12:
                 bestC=C; current=cand.copy(); stalls=0; break
         else:
             stalls +=1
      if stalls>=4: break
   return current,bestC
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import functools
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog


def corr_A_full(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size < 2:
        return np.array([max(0.0, float(h[0]) * (1.0 - h[0])) if h.size else 0.0])
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return c[::-1]


def compute_c5_full(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(h.size)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c) * dx)


def build_full_period(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    return np.concatenate((a, a))


def compute_c5_period(a):
    return compute_c5_full(build_full_period(a))


def fix_sum_exact(h, target):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = float(target)

    for _ in range(80):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < 1.0
        else:
            mask = h > 0.0
        if not np.any(mask):
            break
        per = diff / float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        h[mask] += per
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)

    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        mask = (h < 1.0) if diff > 0.0 else (h > 0.0)
        if np.any(mask):
            idx = np.where(mask)[0]
            for i in idx:
                if abs(diff) <= 1e-14:
                    break
                if diff > 0.0:
                    amt = min(float(diff), 1.0 - float(h[i]))
                else:
                    amt = max(float(diff), -float(h[i]))
                h[i] += amt
                diff = target - float(h.sum())
    return h


def upsample_to(h_old, n_new):
    h_old = np.asarray(h_old, dtype=float)
    n_old = h_old.size
    if n_old == n_new:
        return fix_sum_exact(h_old, 0.5 * n_new)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new, endpoint=False)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_old)
    return fix_sum_exact(y, 0.5 * n_new)


def upsample_period(a_old, m_new):
    a_old = np.asarray(a_old, dtype=float)
    m_old = a_old.size
    if m_old == m_new:
        return fix_sum_exact(a_old, 0.5 * m_new)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m_old, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m_new, endpoint=False)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a_old)
    return fix_sum_exact(y, 0.5 * m_new)


def soft_value_weights(A, T):
    A = np.asarray(A, dtype=float)
    n = A.size
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([])
    if T <= 1e-8:
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        p[int(np.argmax(A))] = 1.0
        return float(A[p > 0]), p
    Amax = float(A.max())
    x = (A - Amax) / T
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        p[int(np.argmax(A))] = 1.0
        return float(A[p > 0]), p
    p = e / s
    V = float(T * (Amax + np.log(s)))
    return V, p


def grad_direct(p, h):
    n = h.size
    N = n - 1
    G = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    p0 = float(p[N])
    if p0 > 1e-15:
        G += p0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)

    for s in range(1, n):
        ps = float(p[N + s])
        if ps > 1e-15:
            m = n - s
            G[:m] += ps * (1.0 - h[s:])
            G[s:] += -ps * h[:m]

        ps = float(p[N - s])
        if ps > 1e-15:
            m = n - s
            G[s:] += ps * (1.0 - h[:m])
            G[:m] += -ps * h[s:]

    return G


def grad_fft(p, h):
    n = h.size
    N = n - 1
    M = 2 * n - 1
    need = M + n - 1
    L = 1 << int(need).bit_length()

    P = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)

    f1 = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    f1[:M] = P
    fH = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    fH[:n] = h
    conv1 = np.fft.fft(f1) * np.fft.fft(fH)
    conv1 = np.fft.ifft(conv1).real
    Hminus = conv1[N:N + n]

    f2 = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    f2[:M] = P[::-1]
    conv2 = np.fft.fft(f2) * np.fft.fft(fH)
    conv2 = np.fft.ifft(conv2).real
    Hplus = conv2[N:N + n]

    prefix = np.empty(M + 1, dtype=float)
    prefix[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(P, out=prefix[1:])
    starts = N - np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    ends = 2 * N - np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    Lval = prefix[ends + 1] - prefix[starts]

    G = Lval - Hplus - Hminus
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)):
        return grad_direct(p, h)
    return G


def smooth_value_grad_full(x, T):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    A = corr_A_full(x)
    V, p = soft_value_weights(A, T)
    if x.size <= 450:
        G = grad_direct(p, x)
    else:
        G = grad_fft(p, x)
    return V, G


def smooth_value_grad_period(a, T):
    m = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).size
    full = np.concatenate((a, a))
    V, G = smooth_value_grad_full(full, T)
    return V, G[:m] + G[m:]


def penalty_obj_full(x, T, lam):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5 * x.size
    V, G = smooth_value_grad_full(x, T)
    delta = float(x.sum()) - target
    val = float(V) + float(lam) * delta * delta
    grad = G + (2.0 * float(lam) * delta) * np.ones_like(x)
    return val, grad


def penalty_obj_period(a, T, lam):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5 * a.size
    V, G = smooth_value_grad_period(a, T)
    delta = float(a.sum()) - target
    val = float(V) + float(lam) * delta * delta
    grad = G + (2.0 * float(lam) * delta) * np.ones_like(a)
    return val, grad


def slsqp_obj_full(x, T):
    return smooth_value_grad_full(x, T)[0]


def slsqp_jac_full(x, T):
    return smooth_value_grad_full(x, T)[1]


def slsqp_obj_period(a, T):
    return smooth_value_grad_period(a, T)[0]


def slsqp_jac_period(a, T):
    return smooth_value_grad_period(a, T)[1]


def eq_fun(x, n):
    return float(np.asarray(x, dtype=float).sum() - 0.5 * float(n))


def eq_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones((1, int(n)), dtype=float)


def periodize_full(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return None
    m = n // 2
    a = 0.5 * (h[:m] + h[m:])
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0)
    return fix_sum_exact(a, 0.5 * m)


def best_period_candidates(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return None, None
    m = n // 2
    cands = []

    a = 0.5 * (h[:m] + h[m:])
    cands.append(fix_sum_exact(np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0), 0.5 * m))

    cands.append(fix_sum_exact(np.clip(h[:m], 0.0, 1.0), 0.5 * m))
    cands.append(fix_sum_exact(np.clip(h[m:], 0.0, 1.0), 0.5 * m))

    best_a = None
    best_c = None
    seen = []
    for a in cands:
        key = tuple(np.round(a, 12))
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.append(key)
        c = compute_c5_period(a)
        if best_c is None or c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_a = a.copy()
    return best_a, best_c


def make_cosine_period(m):
    if m < 2:
        m = max(2, m)
        x = np.arange(m)
    a = 0.5 + np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * np.arange(m) / float(m))
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0)
    return fix_sum_exact(a, 0.5 * m)


def make_random_binary(m, rng):
    if m % 2 != 0:
        m = m + 1
    a = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    a[:m // 2] = 1.0
    rng.shuffle(a)
    return a


def polish_full(h0, time_limit, current_best_c, rng):
    h0 = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float)
    n = h0.size
    t0 = time.time()
    if n < 2 or time_limit < 10.0:
        return h0.copy(), current_best_c

    current = h0.copy()
    best_c = float(current_best_c)
    target = 0.5 * n
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n

    if n <= 200:
        maxiter_lbfgs = 1600
        maxiter_slsqp = 700
    elif n <= 450:
        maxiter_lbfgs = 900
        maxiter_slsqp = 400
    else:
        maxiter_lbfgs = 450
        maxiter_slsqp = 0

    if time_limit > 220.0:
        T_list = [10.0, 5.0, 2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.2, 0.1]
    elif time_limit > 110.0:
        T_list = [5.0, 2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.2]
    else:
        T_list = [2.0, 1.0, 0.5]

    for T in T_list:
        if time.time() - t0 > time_limit * 0.65:
            break
        try:
            fun = functools.partial(penalty_obj_full, T=T, lam=1e3)
            res = minimize(
                fun, current,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": maxiter_lbfgs, "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-8, "maxls": 20}
            )
            cand = fix_sum_exact(np.clip(res.x, 0.0, 1.0), target)
            c = compute_c5_full(cand)
            if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = c
                current = cand.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    if maxiter_slsqp > 0 and time.time() - t0 < time_limit * 0.88:
        for T in [2.0, 1.0, 0.5]:
            if time.time() - t0 > time_limit * 0.92:
                break
            try:
                obj = functools.partial(slsqp_obj_full, T=T)
                jac = functools.partial(slsqp_jac_full, T=T)
                cons = [{
                    "type": "eq",
                    "fun": functools.partial(eq_fun, n=n),
                    "jac": functools.partial(eq_jac, n=n)
                }]
                res = minimize(
                    obj, current,
                    jac=jac,
                    method="SLSQP",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    constraints=cons,
                    options={"maxiter": maxiter_slsqp, "ftol": 1e-12, "disp": False}
                )
                cand = fix_sum_exact(np.clip(res.x, 0.0, 1.0), target)
                c = compute_c5_full(cand)
                if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = c
                    current = cand.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    if time.time() - t0 < time_limit:
        n_restart = 2 if n <= 250 else 1
        for r in range(n_restart):
            if time.time() - t0 > time_limit:
                break
            x = current + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, n)
            x = fix_sum_exact(np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0), target)
            try:
                fun = functools.partial(penalty_obj_full, T=0.5, lam=1e3)
                maxit_p = max(50, maxiter_lbfgs // 4)
                res = minimize(
                    fun, x,
                    jac=True,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options={"maxiter": maxit_p, "ftol": 1e-11, "gtol": 1e-7}
                )
                cand = fix_sum_exact(np.clip(res.x, 0.0, 1.0), target)
                c = compute_c5_full(cand)
                if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = c
                    current = cand.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    return current, best_c


def polish_period(a0, time_limit, current_best_c, rng):
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=float)
    m = a0.size
    t0 = time.time()
    if m < 2 or time_limit < 10.0:
        return a0.copy(), current_best_c

    current = a0.copy()
    best_c = float(current_best_c)
    target = 0.5 * m
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * m

    if m <= 160:
        maxiter_lbfgs = 1800
        maxiter_slsqp = 700
    elif m <= 360:
        maxiter_lbfgs = 1000
        maxiter_slsqp = 400
    else:
        maxiter_lbfgs = 500
        maxiter_slsqp = 0

    if time_limit > 220.0:
        T_list = [10.0, 5.0, 2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.2, 0.1]
    elif time_limit > 110.0:
        T_list = [5.0, 2.0, 1.0, 0.5, 0.2]
    else:
        T_list = [2.0, 1.0, 0.5]

    for T in T_list:
        if time.time() - t0 > time_limit * 0.65:
            break
        try:
            fun = functools.partial(penalty_obj_period, T=T, lam=1e3)
            res = minimize(
                fun, current,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": maxiter_lbfgs, "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-8, "maxls": 20}
            )
            cand = fix_sum_exact(np.clip(res.x, 0.0, 1.0), target)
            c = compute_c5_period(cand)
            if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = c
                current = cand.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    if maxiter_slsqp > 0 and m <= 420 and time.time() - t0 < time_limit * 0.88:
        for T in [2.0, 1.0, 0.5]:
            if time.time() - t0 > time_limit * 0.92:
                break
            try:
                obj = functools.partial(slsqp_obj_period, T=T)
                jac = functools.partial(slsqp_jac_period, T=T)
                cons = [{
                    "type": "eq",
                    "fun": functools.partial(eq_fun, n=m),
                    "jac": functools.partial(eq_jac, n=m)
                }]
                res = minimize(
                    obj, current,
                    jac=jac,
                    method="SLSQP",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    constraints=cons,
                    options={"maxiter": maxiter_slsqp, "ftol": 1e-12, "disp": False}
                )
                cand = fix_sum_exact(np.clip(res.x, 0.0, 1.0), target)
                c = compute_c5_period(cand)
                if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = c
                    current = cand.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    if time.time() - t0 < time_limit:
        n_restart = 2 if m <= 200 else 1
        for r in range(n_restart):
            if time.time() - t0 > time_limit:
                break
            x = current + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, m)
            x = fix_sum_exact(np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0), target)
            try:
                fun = functools.partial(penalty_obj_period, T=0.5, lam=1e3)
                maxit_p = max(50, maxiter_lbfgs // 4)
                res = minimize(
                    fun, x,
                    jac=True,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options={"maxiter": maxit_p, "ftol": 1e-11, "gtol": 1e-7}
                )
                cand = fix_sum_exact(np.clip(res.x, 0.0, 1.0), target)
                c = compute_c5_period(cand)
                if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = c
                    current = cand.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    return current, best_c


def grad_lag_int(d_idx, h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    N = n - 1
    s = int(d_idx) - N
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    if s == 0:
        g[:] = 1.0 - 2.0 * h
    elif s > 0:
        m = n - s
        g[:m] += 1.0 - h[s:]
        g[s:] += -h[:m]
    else:
        sv = -s
        m = n - sv
        g[sv:] += 1.0 - h[:m]
        g[:m] += -h[sv:]
    return g


def linear_polish_full(h0, time_limit, current_best_c):
    h0 = np.asarray(h0, dtype=float)
    n = h0.size
    if n < 10 or n > 650 or time_limit < 20.0:
        return h0.copy(), current_best_c

    t0 = time.time()
    current = h0.copy()
    best_c = float(current_best_c)
    target = 0.5 * n

    if n <= 220:
        K = 48
        rounds = 28
    elif n <= 420:
        K = 28
        rounds = 18
    else:
        K = 16
        rounds = 10

    stalls = 0
    for _ in range(rounds):
        if time.time() - t0 > time_limit * 0.9:
            break
        A = corr_A_full(current)
        idx = np.argsort(A)[::-1][:K]
        rows = []
        bubs = []
        for d_idx in idx:
            rows.append(grad_lag_int(d_idx, current))
            bubs.append(-float(A[d_idx]))

        A_arr = np.vstack(rows)
        A_ub = np.column_stack((A_arr, -np.ones(A_arr.shape[0], dtype=float)))
        b_ub = np.array(bubs, dtype=float)

        cvec = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
        cvec[-1] = 1.0
        A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=float)
        A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
        b_eq = np.zeros(1, dtype=float)

        bounds = [(-float(current[i]), 1.0 - float(current[i])) for i in range(n)]
        bounds.append((None, None))

        try:
            res = linprog(
                cvec,
                A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs"
            )
        except Exception:
            try:
                res = linprog(
                    cvec,
                    A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub,
                    A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    method="simplex"
                )
            except Exception:
                break

        if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
            stalls += 1
        else:
            z = np.asarray(res.x[:-1], dtype=float)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
                stalls += 1
                continue
            improved = False
            for scale in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1):
                cand = fix_sum_exact(np.clip(current + scale * z, 0.0, 1.0), target)
                c = compute_c5_full(cand)
                if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = c
                    current = cand.copy()
                    improved = True
                    break
            if not improved:
                stalls += 1
        if stalls >= 4:
            break

    return current, best_c


def linear_polish_period(a0, time_limit, current_best_c):
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=float)
    m = a0.size
    if m < 6 or m > 350 or time_limit < 15.0:
        return a0.copy(), current_best_c

    t0 = time.time()
    current = a0.copy()
    best_c = float(current_best_c)
    target = 0.5 * m

    if m <= 160:
        K = 36
        rounds = 22
    else:
        K = 18
        rounds = 10

    stalls = 0
    for _ in range(rounds):
        if time.time() - t0 > time_limit * 0.9:
            break
        full = build_full_period(current)
        A = corr_A_full(full)
        idx = np.argsort(A)[::-1][:K]
        rows = []
        bubs = []
        for d_idx in idx:
            gf = grad_lag_int(d_idx, full)
            rows.append(gf[:m] + gf[m:])
            bubs.append(-float(A[d_idx]))

        A_arr = np.vstack(rows)
        A_ub = np.column_stack((A_arr, -np.ones(A_arr.shape[0], dtype=float)))
        b_ub = np.array(bubs, dtype=float)

        cvec = np.zeros(m + 1, dtype=float)
        cvec[-1] = 1.0
        A_eq = np.zeros((1, m + 1), dtype=float)
        A_eq[0, :m] = 1.0
        b_eq = np.zeros(1, dtype=float)

        bounds = [(-float(current[i]), 1.0 - float(current[i])) for i in range(m)]
        bounds.append((None, None))

        try:
            res = linprog(
                cvec,
                A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs"
            )
        except Exception:
            try:
                res = linprog(
                    cvec,
                    A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub,
                    A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    method="simplex"
                )
            except Exception:
                break

        if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
            stalls += 1
        else:
            z = np.asarray(res.x[:-1], dtype=float)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
                stalls += 1
                continue
            improved = False
            for scale in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1):
                cand = fix_sum_exact(np.clip(current + scale * z, 0.0, 1.0), target)
                c = compute_c5_period(cand)
                if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = c
                    current = cand.copy()
                    improved = True
                    break
            if not improved:
                stalls += 1
        if stalls >= 4:
            break

    return current, best_c


def binarize_attempt(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return h
    order = np.argsort(h)
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    b[order[n // 2:]] = 1.0
    return fix_sum_exact(b, 0.5 * n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.time()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = globals().get("initial_h_values", None)
    if parent is not None and np.size(parent) > 0:
        h = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float).copy()
    else:
        h = make_cosine_period(90)
        h = build_full_period(h)

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    h = fix_sum_exact(h, 0.5 * h.size)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5_full(best_h)

    hard_deadline = t_start + max(5.0, 0.95 * float(budget_s))

    def remain():
        return max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.time())

    # Initial-stage local optimization at parent resolution.
    n0 = best_h.size
    if remain() > 200.0:
        tlim = min(remain() * 0.25, 260.0)
        h2, c2 = polish_full(best_h, tlim * 0.55, best_c, rng)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()

    if n0 % 2 == 0 and remain() > 140.0:
        a0, c0 = best_period_candidates(best_h)
        if a0 is not None:
            tlim = min(remain() * 0.18, 170.0)
            a2, c2 = polish_period(a0, tlim, best_c, rng)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = build_full_period(a2)
                best_h = fix_sum_exact(best_h, 0.5 * best_h.size)

    # Multi-resolution full-space polish.
    base_n = n0
    res_set = set()
    for mult in (1, 2, 4, 6):
        nn = int(base_n * mult)
        if nn < 20:
            nn = 20
        if nn % 2 != 0:
            nn += 1
        if nn > 1080:
            nn = 1080
        res_set.add(nn)
    resolutions = sorted(res_set)

    for nnew in resolutions:
        if remain() < 120.0:
            break
        cap = 260.0 if nnew <= 200 else (430.0 if nnew <= 450 else 560.0)
        alloc = min(remain() * 0.30, cap)
        if alloc < 60.0:
            break

        x0 = upsample_to(best_h, nnew)

        h2, c2 = polish_full(x0, alloc * 0.58, best_c, rng)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()

        if nnew % 2 == 0 and remain() < hard_deadline:
            mnew = nnew // 2
            period_starts = []
            a_from, c_from = best_period_candidates(h2 if c2 <= best_c else best_h)
            if a_from is not None:
                period_starts.append(a_from)
            period_starts.append(make_cosine_period(mnew))

            used = 0.0
            for a0 in period_starts:
                if remain() < 80.0:
                    break
                share = max(45.0, (alloc * 0.42 - used) / max(1, len(period_starts) - len(period_starts[:0]) - 1 + 1))
                share = min(share, alloc * 0.42 - used, remain() * 0.6)
                if share < 25.0:
                    break
                a2, c2 = polish_period(a0, share, best_c, rng)
                used += min(share, alloc * 0.42)
                if c2 < best_c:
                    best_c = c2
                    best_h = build_full_period(a2)
                    best_h = fix_sum_exact(best_h, 0.5 * best_h.size)

    # Final refinement at the best resolution found.
    if remain() > 80.0:
        tlim = min(remain() * 0.25, 220.0)
        h2, c2 = polish_full(best_h, tlim, best_c, rng)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()

    if remain() > 60.0 and best_h.size % 2 == 0:
        a0, _ = best_period_candidates(best_h)
        if a0 is not None:
            tlim = min(remain() * 0.20, 170.0)
            a2, c2 = polish_period(a0, tlim, best_c, rng)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = build_full_period(a2)
                best_h = fix_sum_exact(best_h, 0.5 * best_h.size)

    if remain() > 45.0:
        tlim = min(remain() * 0.20, 160.0)
        h2, c2 = linear_polish_full(best_h, tlim, best_c)
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()

    if remain() > 30.0 and best_h.size % 2 == 0:
        a0, _ = best_period_candidates(best_h)
        if a0 is not None:
            tlim = min(remain() * 0.15, 100.0)
            a2, c2 = linear_polish_period(a0, tlim, best_c)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = build_full_period(a2)
                best_h = fix_sum_exact(best_h, 0.5 * best_h.size)

    # A cheap exact binary threshold attempt.
    if best_h.size % 2 == 0 and remain() > 10.0:
        b = binarize_attempt(best_h)
        cb = compute_c5_full(b)
        if cb < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = cb
            best_h = b.copy()

    # Final feasibility and verification.
    best_h = np.clip(best_h, 0.0, 1.0)
    best_h = fix_sum_exact(best_h, 0.5 * best_h.size)
    best_c = compute_c5_full(best_h)

    return (best_h, best_c, int(best_h.size))
```
